# Household hh_s151 — 32 days, Monday to Thursday

Seed-generated household. Times are clock times; ids are the receptacle and object ids used in events.jsonl. A line indented under an activity says where an object went when the activity ended and why. WHIM marks a placement that landed somewhere other than where the decision was heading. Text in [brackets] names the hidden cause the decision is attributed to.

## Household

Type: flatmates. Rooms: bathroom, bedroom_1, bedroom_2, dining, entry, kitchen, living, office.

Residents:

- **Dana** (resident_1): works outside the home; fairly tidy (tidiness 0.52); erratic timing (jitter scale 1.57); sometimes forgets pocket items; mood strongly affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.79); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the bedroom_1 desk. Hobbies: cycling, family_calls, yoga. Chores they take on: dusting, fixing, grocery_run, vacuuming.
- **Felix** (resident_2): a student with classes out; fairly tidy (tidiness 0.64); erratic timing (jitter scale 1.73); sometimes forgets pocket items; mood strongly affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.81); sleeps in bedroom_2, works at the office desk. Hobbies: family_calls, movies, snacking. Chores they take on: dusting, fixing, grocery_run, vacuuming.

Pet: a dog, mainly looked after by Dana.
